检查原因与下一步

  • 已保留的原始候选问题：论点的引用类型不满足所选判断范围。记录了某人的说法，不等于证实了所述事件。

    原论点：The presence of a fraud flag on the rider profile elevates the required agent confidence to 95% under section 6.3, which cannot be satisfied amidst unresolved threshold clock conflicts, necessitating human review.

    opening-rider-claims-3

    下一步：核对原论点的每个事实部分及原始记录，补齐相关依据或保留证据不足。

    ARGUMENT_ASSESSMENT_WITHOUT_RECORD_SUPPORT

  • 已保留的原始候选问题：论点的引用类型不满足所选判断范围。记录了某人的说法，不等于证实了所述事件。

    原论点：Because a fraud flag is recorded on the rider profile, policy v2.1 Section 6.3 establishes a 95% confidence requirement that cannot be satisfied while policy source precedence and threshold start clocks remain unresolved.

    response-rider-claims-3

    下一步：核对原论点的每个事实部分及原始记录，补齐相关依据或保留证据不足。

    ARGUMENT_ASSESSMENT_WITHOUT_RECORD_SUPPORT

  • 正常人工处理原因：政策与样例规则的关系尚未明确。

    下一步：先确认适用规则，再决定是否能够自动处理。

    policy_clarification

本次结果

退款金额：未裁定 SGD · 页面只展示建议，不执行收费或退款。

  • Unresolved conflict of precedence between embedded policy P-004 mandating an 8-minute no-show threshold and standalone policy v2.1 Section 2.1 establishing a 5-minute free wait from scheduled pickup.
  • Unspecified trigger event and policy clock governing embedded policy rule P-004 (whether measured from driver arrival, driver wait start, or scheduled pickup).
  • Escalation is mandated by unresolved policy source precedence between embedded P-004 and standalone v2.1 Section 2.1, coupled with the Section 6.3 95% confidence threshold triggered by the rider's fraud flag.

已记录的依据

  • 暂无可采纳的裁决依据。

计算结果与待澄清问题

算术结果与已明确的规则分开列出；未明示的收费条件仍需澄清。距离／差额不等于责任／退款。

  • trip_wait480 秒 / 8 分钟E-005 E-006 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039
  • app_wait_5470 秒 / 7.83333 分钟E-006 E-028 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039
  • scheduled_pickup_to_cancellation360 秒 / 6 分钟E-003 E-006 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039
  • 政策免费等待从预约时间起 5 分钟；结束：2026-09-13T08:50:00+08:00P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • 仅供提示 · WAIT_START_DISAGREEMENT

    Order and app timer start records produce different elapsed times. Preserve both calculations. Section 2.1 explicitly starts the free wait at scheduled pickup; the logged timers remain separate records.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • 仅供提示 · WAIT_START_BEFORE_SCHEDULED_PICKUP

    Recorded wait starts precede the scheduled pickup; whether early waiting counts is not established by the data. Section 2.1 explicitly starts the free wait at scheduled pickup; the logged timers remain separate records.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • 必须补充政策或数据 · NO_SHOW_THRESHOLD_CLOCK_UNSPECIFIED

    The embedded no-show threshold has no defined start event. Section 2.1 defines free wait from scheduled pickup but does not define this separate threshold clock.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • 必须补充政策或数据 · POLICY_SOURCE_CONFLICT

    Embedded cancellation thresholds/fee differ from standalone v2.1 sections 2.1-2.2 (cancel after 5 free minutes from scheduled pickup; $5 fee). Source precedence is not supplied. Do not treat 5+8 as 13 or choose the favorable clock.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
  • 仅供提示 · FRAUD_FLAG_CONFIDENCE_REQUIREMENT

    Section 6.3 raises the required confidence to 95%. This is not proof of present fault and no confidence value is fabricated.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3

Judge 如何看双方观点

以下为通过结构和引用检查的分析；这些检查不能证明自然语言推理正确。

核心问题

Central dispute: Whether the driver properly satisfied no-show cancellation requirements to uphold the $5.00 cancellation fee, or whether an unresolved conflict between the embedded 8-minute no-show threshold and standalone Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (along with the 95% confidence requirement under Section 6.3) mandates human escalation.

乘客观点 · 部分有依据支持

The rider's arguments regarding the existence of an unresolved threshold clock conflict, mathematical elapsed time from scheduled pickup, and the elevated 95% confidence threshold under Section 6.3 are supported by records and audit findings, whereas the factual claim of driver absence is contradicted by telemetry and arrival records.

涉及论点：opening-rider-claims-0 opening-rider-claims-1 opening-rider-claims-2 opening-rider-claims-3 opening-rider-counterpoints-0 opening-rider-counterpoints-1 response-rider-claims-0 response-rider-claims-1 response-rider-claims-2 response-rider-claims-3 response-rider-counterpoints-0 response-rider-counterpoints-1 response-rider-counterpoints-2

依据：E-003 E-006 E-033 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3

司机观点 · 部分有依据支持

The driver's claims of physical arrival at 08:43:00, pre-cancellation contact compliance under Section 2.3, and waiting 360 seconds past scheduled pickup are supported by system logs, but the claim of definitive fee entitlement cannot be accepted due to unresolved policy source precedence with embedded rule P-004.

涉及论点：opening-driver-claims-0 opening-driver-claims-1 opening-driver-claims-2 opening-driver-claims-3 opening-driver-counterpoints-0 opening-driver-counterpoints-1 opening-driver-counterpoints-2 response-driver-claims-0 response-driver-claims-1 response-driver-claims-2 response-driver-claims-3 response-driver-counterpoints-0 response-driver-counterpoints-1

依据：E-001 E-004 E-013 E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-026 E-029 E-035 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3

逐条论点评估

  • opening-rider-claims-0 缺少支持证据

    Original assertion: The rider stated they waited 10 minutes at the lobby and the driver was absent. Attribution support: dispute ticket description E-002 records the statement. Incident support/opposition: Telemetry records E-001 and arrival event E-026 contradict driver absence; no independent evidence proves rider presence. Policy consequence: A party statement alone does not verify the incident.

    支持记录：无 · 反驳记录：E-001 E-026

    政策：P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4

    待补充：Independent verification of rider physical presence and visibility at the pickup location.

  • opening-rider-claims-1 有支持

    Original assertion: A material policy conflict exists between embedded rule P-004 (8-minute threshold) and policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (5 minutes free wait from scheduled pickup). Audit and calculations confirm the coexistence of conflicting rules without a supplied precedence rule.

裁决关键条件

  • no_show_charge · 未知

    Fixed fee entitlement cannot be definitively determined due to unresolved precedence between embedded rule P-004 and standalone Policy v2.1 Section 2.1, and unspecified start clock for P-004.

    E-003 E-005 E-006 E-008 E-031 E-035 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
  • free_wait · 满足

    Under Policy v2.1 Section 2.1, the 5-minute free wait elapsed from scheduled pickup (08:45:00) to 08:50:00, with cancellation occurring at 08:51:00 (360 seconds elapsed).

    E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • driver_contact · 满足

    The driver sent four in-app chat messages and initiated an in-app call ringing 22 seconds before cancellation, fully satisfying Section 2.3 contact requirements.

    E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
  • driver_arrival · 满足

    Driver arrived at 08:43:00, prior to 08:45:00 scheduled pickup, with system telemetry confirming arrival within 10 meters of pickup coordinates at speed 0 km/h.

    E-001 E-004 E-013 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4

争议点分析

  • Governing no-show waiting clock and threshold precedence

    Embedded cancellation policy P-004 specifies an 8-minute no-show threshold, whereas Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 permits cancellation after 5 minutes of free wait from scheduled pickup. The trip cancelled 360 seconds (6 minutes) after scheduled pickup and 480 seconds (8 minutes) after driver arrival/wait start. Without an authoritative policy rule governing clock precedence, this discrepancy cannot be resolved by an automated decision.

    E-003 E-005 E-006 E-031 E-035 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
  • Driver arrival and lobby presence

    The rider stated the driver was absent at the pickup point, but GPS telemetry and auto-arrival event records verify the vehicle arrived within 10 meters of the pickup point at 08:43:00 at 0 km/h, contradicting the assertion that the driver never arrived.

    E-001 E-002 E-004 E-013 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4

复核问题逐项处理

  • NO_SHOW_THRESHOLD_CLOCK_UNSPECIFIED · 仍待澄清

    Embedded policy rule P-004 references an 8-minute threshold without defining whether it triggers from driver arrival or scheduled pickup. Standalone Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 anchors free wait to scheduled pickup but does not define the separate embedded threshold clock.

    E-003 E-005 E-006 E-031 E-035 E-037 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • POLICY_SOURCE_CONFLICT · 仍待澄清

    Embedded cancellation policy P-004 mandates an 8-minute no-show threshold while standalone Policy v2.1 Sections 2.1-2.2 permit cancellation after 5 minutes of free wait starting from scheduled pickup. Hierarchy between dataset policy and standalone policy is unspecified and cannot be inferred.

    E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2

仍需补充的信息

  • Official operational guidance or precedence ruling determining whether embedded rule P-004 (8 minutes) or standalone Policy v2.1 §2.1 (5 minutes from scheduled pickup) controls no-show cancellation fee entitlement.
  • Authoritative clock definition establishing the triggering start event for the 8-minute no-show threshold referenced in P-004 and app event E-031.

先例对照

  • P-RYDE-2.1-S7.3

    相似点：Both disputes involve a $5.00 no-show cancellation fee applied after driver waiting at pickup.

    差异：In precedent DISP-2026-0211, the driver sent zero messages and made zero calls, violating Section 2.3. In the present case, the driver fully satisfied Section 2.3 contact requirements by sending four messages and placing an in-app call.

本次绑定的政策与程序检查

RYDE-POLICY-V2.1 · 生效 2026-01-01

人工审核门槛

  • NO_SHOW_THRESHOLD_CLOCK_UNSPECIFIED The embedded no-show threshold has no defined start event. Section 2.1 defines free wait from scheduled pickup but does not define this separate threshold clock. P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • POLICY_SOURCE_CONFLICT Embedded cancellation thresholds/fee differ from standalone v2.1 sections 2.1-2.2 (cancel after 5 free minutes from scheduled pickup; $5 fee). Source precedence is not supplied. Do not treat 5+8 as 13 or choose the favorable clock. P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2

逐项政策条件

  • no_show_charge · unknown

    Fixed fee requires reconciled policy, sufficient scheduled free wait, arrival and both contact attempts.

    E-001 E-003 E-004 E-005 E-006 E-007 E-008 E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-022 E-023 E-024 E-025 E-026 E-027 E-028 E-029 E-030 E-031 E-032 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-038 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • free_wait · met

    Five free minutes start at scheduled pickup; exact-boundary cancellation requires clarification.

    E-003 E-006 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • driver_contact · met

    At least one in-app message AND one call attempt strictly before cancellation. Missing logs alone do not prove no attempt; conflicting complete summaries require review.

    E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
  • driver_arrival · met

    Recorded arrival event corroborates timely arrival; driver messages alone are not location evidence.

    E-004 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4

程序保守修正

程序保留原输出，对不满足来源要求的论点降低支持评级，或按逐条评价修正汇总标签，然后重新检查全部规则。Fare 仅在政策条件及主依据独立成立时允许此修正；裁决与金额不变，未执行任何收费或退款。

双方陈述与回应

offline_placeholder 只摘录已有陈述，不代表已完成真实 Agent 辩论。

乘客 Advocate

第一轮陈述

  • The rider stated they were present at the Tiong Bahru Plaza lobby on time, waited 10 minutes, did not see the vehicle, and found the driver absent. E-002 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • A material policy conflict exists between embedded rule P-004 requiring an 8-minute no-show threshold and policy v2.1 section 2.1 allowing cancellation after 5 minutes of free wait starting at scheduled pickup. E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • From the scheduled pickup time of 08:45:00 to cancellation at 08:51:00, only 360 seconds (6 minutes) elapsed, failing to satisfy the 8-minute no-show threshold if measured from scheduled pickup. E-003 E-006 E-037 P-004
  • The presence of a fraud flag on the rider profile elevates the required agent confidence to 95% under section 6.3, which cannot be satisfied amidst unresolved threshold clock conflicts, necessitating human review. E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3
  • System logs show the driver sent four chat messages and placed an unanswered call ringing for 22 seconds between 08:43:00 and 08:50:45, satisfying the contact prerequisites of section 2.3. E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
  • GPS samples record the driver at the pickup coordinates with speed 0 km/h from 08:43:00 to 08:51:00, though intermittent samples do not establish uninterrupted presence or visibility at the lobby entrance. E-001 E-013 E-014 E-015 E-016 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • Resolution of the policy conflict between embedded cancellation threshold P-004 (8 minutes) and policy v2.1 section 2.1 (5 minutes from scheduled pickup).
  • The governing start clock and definition for the 8-minute no-show threshold specified in P-004 and app event E-031.
  • Verification of rider app notification delivery and receipt status for driver messages and the in-app arrival alert.

回应对方

  • The rider reported waiting at the Tiong Bahru Plaza lobby on time for 10 minutes without finding the vehicle, asserting that the driver was not present and requesting reversal of the $5.00 fee. E-002 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • A material conflict exists between embedded policy P-004 mandating an 8-minute no-show threshold and standalone policy v2.1 Section 2.1 permitting cancellation after 5 minutes of free wait from scheduled pickup. E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • From scheduled pickup at 08:45:00 to cancellation at 08:51:00, only 360 seconds (6 minutes) elapsed; if the 8-minute threshold in P-004 governs from scheduled pickup, the cancellation was premature. E-003 E-006 E-037 P-004
  • Because a fraud flag is recorded on the rider profile, policy v2.1 Section 6.3 establishes a 95% confidence requirement that cannot be satisfied while policy source precedence and threshold start clocks remain unresolved. E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3
  • Although system events and telemetry samples record the driver at the pickup coordinates at 08:43:00 with auto-arrival confirmed, discrete GPS samples do not prove continuous presence or physical visibility at the lobby entrance. E-001 E-004 E-013 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • The communication logs confirm the driver sent multiple chat messages and initiated an unanswered call prior to cancellation, satisfying the procedural contact conditions of Section 2.3. E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
  • The driver's 480-second wait from 08:43:00 to 08:51:00 relies on an early arrival timer, whereas Section 2.1 explicitly anchors the free wait period to the 08:45:00 scheduled pickup time. E-003 E-005 E-006 E-035 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • Resolution of hierarchy and precedence between embedded cancellation policy P-004 (8-minute no-show threshold) and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (5-minute free wait from scheduled pickup).
  • Formal definition of the triggering start event and policy clock for embedded policy rule P-004 (driver arrival time vs. scheduled pickup time).
  • Verification of whether the rider received and viewed the driver's in-app messages and call alerts prior to cancellation.

司机 Advocate

第一轮陈述

  • The driver timely arrived at the designated pickup location at 08:43:00, prior to the 08:45:00 scheduled pickup time, as verified by system telemetry and arrival events. E-001 E-004 E-013 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • The driver fulfilled all pre-cancellation contact requirements under Section 2.3 by sending four in-app chat messages and placing an in-app phone call prior to trip cancellation. E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
  • The driver waited past the required 5-minute free wait window ending at 08:50:00, with 360 seconds elapsed between the 08:45:00 scheduled pickup and the 08:51:00 cancellation. E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
  • The driver remained stationary at the pickup coordinates across multiple recorded telemetry samples from 08:43:00 through 08:51:00, totaling 480 seconds from driver wait start. E-001 E-005 E-013 E-014 E-015 E-016 E-035 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
  • The rider stated they were waiting at the lobby and that the driver never appeared, but telemetry logs record the vehicle stationed at the pickup coordinates with automated arrival confirmed. E-001 E-002 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • While discrete GPS points at 08:43, 08:45, 08:48, and 08:51 record speed 0 km/h at the pickup location, they do not establish uninterrupted observation between sample timestamps. E-001
  • A conflict exists regarding whether the 8-minute no-show threshold in embedded policy starts from driver arrival or scheduled pickup, though the 5-minute free wait past scheduled pickup under v2.1 Section 2.1 was fully met. E-003 E-004 E-006 E-031 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • Resolution of the policy conflict between embedded cancellation policy P-004 (8-minute threshold) and Ryde Policy v2.1 §2.1 (cancellation permitted after 5-minute free wait from scheduled pickup).
  • Specification of the triggering start event and policy clock for embedded policy rule P-004 (driver_arrival_time vs. scheduled_pickup_time).

回应对方

  • The driver timely arrived at 08:43:00, prior to the 08:45:00 scheduled pickup, with system telemetry recording the vehicle within 10 meters of the pickup point at 0 km/h and an automatic arrival notification sent to the rider. E-004 E-013 E-026 E-027 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • The driver fully met the pre-cancellation contact obligation under Section 2.3 by transmitting four in-app chat messages describing exact location and vehicle details and placing an in-app call ringing 22 seconds without answer. E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
  • Under Policy v2.1 Section 2.1, the 5-minute free wait elapsed from scheduled pickup (08:45:00 to 08:50:00), making the 08:51:00 cancellation compliant as 360 seconds elapsed, thereby entitling the driver to the $5.00 fee under Section 2.2. E-003 E-006 E-008 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
  • Even under the 8-minute no-show threshold logged in app event E-031, exactly 480 seconds (8 full minutes) elapsed from the recorded driver wait start at 08:43:00 to cancellation at 08:51:00, confirming the driver waited 8 minutes at the pickup coordinates. E-001 E-005 E-006 E-013 E-014 E-015 E-016 E-031 E-035 P-001 P-002 P-004
  • While the rider reported waiting 10 minutes at the lobby without seeing the driver, recorded GPS samples and app events establish the driver was stopped within 10 meters of the pickup point from 08:43:00 through 08:51:00 and proactively messaged the vehicle description. E-001 E-002 E-018 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
  • The rider advocate asserts an unresolved conflict between the 8-minute embedded threshold and the 5-minute Section 2.1 rule; however, the driver satisfied both Section 2.1 (waiting past 5 minutes from scheduled pickup) and the app-triggered 8-minute threshold measured from wait start at 08:43:00. E-005 E-006 E-031 E-035 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
  • Resolution of rule precedence between embedded policy P-004 (8-minute threshold) and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (5-minute free wait from scheduled pickup).
  • Explicit definition of the start event governing the 8-minute no-show threshold referenced in P-004 and app event E-031.
  • System confirmation of delivery and display status on the rider device for the driver's four in-app chat messages and arrival alert.
